- **Probe #335 (`P317` · `P317-SEMICONDUCTOR-PLASMA-ETCH-RIE-ICP-ALE-CF4-C4F8-NF3-CL2-HBR-BCL3-AND-ESC-HELIUM-COOLING-NFPA318-SEMIS2-SEMIS6-OSHA1910119-OSHA19101000-TOXIC-CL2-HBR-BCL3-RFO-TRIP-ESC-HE-LEAK-RF-QUENCH-AND-POLYMER-HF-CL2-SCBA-LOTO-GUARD`)**: Semiconductor Dry Plasma Etch (`RIE / ICP / ALE` Fluorocarbon & Chlorine/Bromine Etchants `CF4 / C4F8 / NF3 / Cl2 / HBr / BCl3` & Electrostatic Chuck `ESC` Helium Backside Cooling) (`NFPA 318 / SEMI S2 / SEMI S6 / OSHA 1910.119 / OSHA 1910.1000`) guard — enforces `0ms` etch gas-box `RFO` valve trip, `RF` plasma quench & `POU` burn-wet scrubber interlock on gas-box chlorine leak `Cl2 >= 0.5 ppm`, `HBr / BCl3 / SiCl4` moisture hydrolysis exhaust `HCl/HBr >= 2.0 ppm`, or `POU` abatement scrubber offline (`trip_etch_gas_box_rfo_quench_rf_plasma_and_interlock_pou_burn_wet_scrubber_in_0ms`), quenches `RF` source/bias power, cuts `ESC` helium backside supply, and holds wafer de-chuck on `ESC` helium leak `> 15.0 sccm` or wafer chuck surface temperature `> 120.0 °C` (`quench_rf_source_bias_power_cut_esc_he_supply_and_hold_wafer_dechuck_sequence`), and locks the etch chamber lid until fluorocarbon/chlorine polymer in-situ `O2` purge and `HF / Cl2` `SCBA + LOTO` are verified (`lock_etch_chamber_lid_and_require_polymer_o2_purge_and_hf_cl2_scba_loto`).
- **Probe #336 (`P318` · `P318-WEBRTC-SIP-RTP-SMPTE-ST2022-7-SEAMLESS-PROTECTION-SWITCHING-DUAL-PATH-HEADER-OVERREAD-PAYLOAD-HASH-DIVERGENCE-PATH-SKEW-BUFFER-OVERFLOW-AND-SWITCH-FLAP-STORM-GUARD`)**: WebRTC & SIP RTP `SMPTE ST 2022-7` Seamless Protection Switching Dual-Path Redundant RTP Transport guard — enforces `0ms` drop on truncated RTP packet overreads (`< 12 B` or `> remaining UDP datagram bytes`) or Path A / Path B `Payload Type (PT)` mismatch (`drop_st2022_7_truncated_rtp_overread_or_pt_mismatched_packet_in_0ms`), rejects same-`SEQ` Path A / Path B payload hash divergence poisoning or unauthenticated cross-`SSRC` stream splice attacks while pinning the verified path (`reject_st2022_7_divergent_seq_payload_or_cross_ssrc_splice_and_pin_verified_path`), and clamps `> 40.0 ms` path differential delay skew or `> 20 Hz` protection-switch flap storms (`clamp_st2022_7_path_differential_skew_or_dampen_hitless_switch_flap_storm`).
- **Probe #337 (`P319` · `P319-SEMICONDUCTOR-RAPID-THERMAL-PROCESSING-RTP-RTA-RTO-RTN-FLASH-LAMP-ANNEAL-PYROMETER-AND-NH3-SIH4-H2-FORMING-GAS-NFPA318-SEMIS2-SEMIS6-NFPA86-OSHA1910119-H2-NH3-LEL-SIH4-AUTOIGNITION-TRIP-PYROMETER-QUARTZ-SLIP-QUENCH-AND-THYRISTOR-CAPACITOR-DISCHARGE-LOTO-GUARD`)**: Semiconductor Rapid Thermal Processing (`RTP / RTA / RTO / RTN / Flash Lamp Millisecond Anneal`, Multi-Zone Pyrometer & `H2 / NH3 / SiH4` Forming Gas) (`NFPA 318 / SEMI S2 / SEMI S6 / NFPA 86 / OSHA 1910.119`) guard — enforces `0ms` `H2 / NH3 / SiH4` valve trip, SCR lamp power cutoff & `N2` purge flood on exhaust `H2 / NH3 >= 25.0% LEL` or residual chamber `O2 > 50.0 ppm` before `H2 / SiH4` introduction (`trip_rtp_h2_nh3_sih4_gas_valves_cut_lamp_scr_power_and_flood_n2_purge_in_0ms`), quenches the tungsten-halogen / flash-lamp array and aborts the thermal ramp on multi-zone pyrometer temperature spread `> 15.0 °C` or quartz-window cooling water `< 12.0 L/min` (`quench_tungsten_halogen_or_flash_lamp_array_and_abort_thermal_ramp`), and locks the `RTP` chamber door until flash capacitor bleed-down `< 50 V` and chamber cooldown `< 60 °C` `LOTO` are verified (`lock_rtp_chamber_door_and_require_capacitor_50v_bleed_and_60c_cooldown_loto`).
- **Probe #338 (`P320` · `P320-WEBRTC-SIP-RTP-SMPTE-ST2110-10-ST2059-2-IEEE1588-PTPV2-MEDIA-CLOCK-SYNC-ROGUE-GRANDMASTER-SPOOFING-PHASE-STEP-JUMP-CLOCK-CLASS-DEGRADATION-AND-SYNC-MESSAGE-FLOOD-GUARD`)**: WebRTC & SIP RTP `SMPTE ST 2110-10 / ST 2059-2` & `IEEE 1588v2 PTPv2` Precision Media Clock Synchronization guard — enforces `0ms` drop on truncated `PTPv2` message overreads (`< 34 B` or `> remaining packet bytes`) or unallowlisted / unlocked rogue Grandmaster `BMCA` takeover announcements (`drop_st2059_2_ptpv2_truncated_overread_or_rogue_grandmaster_announce_in_0ms`), engages local `OCXO` media clock holdover and slew-limits the RTP timestamp `PLL` on phase step jumps `> 50.0 us` or Grandmaster `clockClass > 187` degradation (`engage_local_ocxo_media_clock_holdover_and_slew_limit_rtp_timestamp_pll`), and throttles `> 256 msg/s` `PTPv2 Sync/Announce` floods while pinning unicast contracts (`rate_limit_ptpv2_sync_announce_flood_and_pin_unicast_negotiation_contract`).
- **Probe #339 (`P321` · `P321-SEMICONDUCTOR-VAPOR-PHASE-EPITAXY-SI-SIGE-SIC-EPI-SIH2CL2-SIHCL3-SIH4-GEH4-B2H6-PH3-ASH3-AND-HCL-CHAMBER-ETCH-NFPA318-SEMIS2-SEMIS6-NFPA86-OSHA1910119-OSHA19101000-CHLOROSILANE-H2-LEL-RFO-TRIP-WATER-LEAK-INDUCTION-QUENCH-AND-HCL-SCBA-LOTO-GUARD`)**: Semiconductor Vapor-Phase Epitaxy (`Si / SiGe / SiC Epi`, `SiH2Cl2 / SiHCl3 / SiH4 / GeH4` Precursors, `B2H6 / PH3 / AsH3` Dopants & High-Temp `HCl` Chamber Etch) (`NFPA 318 / SEMI S2 / SEMI S6 / NFPA 86 / OSHA 1910.119 / OSHA 1910.1000`) guard — enforces `0ms` chlorosilane/hydride/`H2` `RFO` valve trip & `N2` purge flood on chlorosilane or hydride dopant leak `>= 0.5 ppm` or `H2` carrier exhaust `>= 25.0% LEL` (`trip_epi_chlorosilane_hydride_h2_rfo_valves_and_flood_n2_purge_in_0ms`), quenches RF induction / lamp heaters and isolates susceptor cooling water on water leak into vacuum/`H2` ambient or quartz dome temperature `> 1250.0 °C` (`quench_rf_induction_lamp_heaters_and_isolate_susceptor_cooling_water`), and locks the epitaxy chamber until high-temp `HCl` bake/purge and chlorosilane hydrolysis `SCBA + LOTO` are verified (`lock_epi_chamber_and_require_hcl_chlorosilane_hydrolysis_purge_and_scba_loto`).
